Result: 在SkillFeed-Bench上，SkillFeed达到75.1%的top-1检索准确率，比预训练路由基线提高23.1个百分点；在用户档案改变参考技能的查询上，加入档案条件化带来35.1个百分点的增益，证明用户档案在改变技能适用性时最为关键。

Conclusion: 对比实验表明，用户档案对技能路由的影响在档案改变技能适用性时最为显著，验证了将用户档案纳入技能路由的必要性和SkillFeed框架的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of reusable skill repositories makes skill routing a critical capability for large language model (LLM) agents. Existing methods treat routing as task-only semantic matching. However, when users with incompatible constraints issue an identical request, this assumption conflates task relevance with skill suitability: a task-only router can select a semantically plausible skill that is unsuitable for the requesting user. To expose this failure mode, we formulate \textit{personalized skill routing} as profile-conditioned retrieval, in which relevance depends jointly on the task and the user profile. We first introduce a profile-counterfactual benchmark, in which the task is held fixed while changes in the user profile induce changes in the reference skill. We further construct paired counterfactual supervision and propose SkillFeed, a progressive retrieve-and-rerank framework that first establishes task--skill alignment and then learns profile-conditioned discrimination. By retrieving body-level evidence and reranking semantically similar but profile-conflicting candidates, SkillFeed identifies skills that satisfy both task requirements and user constraints. On SkillFeed-Bench, SkillFeed attains 75.1\% top-1 retrieval accuracy, a 23.1-point improvement over the corresponding pretrained routing baseline. Adding profile conditioning yields a 35.1-point gain on queries where user profile changes the reference skill. This contrast shows that user profiles are most consequential precisely when they change skill suitability. Our website is publicly available at http://www.aiskillfeed.com .

</details>


### [173] [Regime-Aware Portfolio Management via Retrieval-Augmented LLM-Guided Expert Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28252)
*Ahmad Asadi,Reza Safabakhsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种检索增强的专家切换框架，通过双流变分自编码器表示资产和市场信息，结合检索知识库和指令微调LLM，在非平稳金融市场中动态选择最优的投资组合管理专家。实验证明该方法在加密货币、股票和外汇市场均优于其他选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场本质上是非平稳的，单一投资组合管理策略的效果高度依赖不断变化的市场条件，需要一种能够根据市场状况动态选择最优专家的方法。

Method: 采用双流变分自编码器分别编码资产级和市场级信息，通过检索知识库存储历史市场情境与专家表现，推理时由指令微调的大语言模型基于检索证据判断最合适的专家，而非直接生成投资组合操作。同时建立了单调性性质，证明加入局部更优的专家不会降低切换机制的绩效。

Result: 在加密货币、股票和外汇三个市场中，所提出的选择器均取得最高的累计收益率和夏普比率。以股票市场为例，累计收益率从最佳固定专家的26%提升至34%，夏普比率从0.74提升至0.96。消融实验证实了检索和LLM推理的重要性，不同专家池规模实验也展示了互补专业知识的价值。

Conclusion: 检索增强的专家切换是应对非平稳金融环境中自适应投资组合管理的有效方法。

Abstract: Financial markets are inherently non-stationary, making the effectiveness of individual portfolio-management strategies highly dependent on changing market conditions. This work proposes a retrieval-augmented expert-switching framework that dynamically selects portfolio management experts based on their historical performance under similar market situations. A dual-stream variational autoencoder represents asset-level and market-wide information, while a retrieval-based knowledge base stores historical situations and expert performance. During inference, an instruction-tuned LLM reasons over the retrieved evidence to identify the most appropriate expert rather than directly generating portfolio actions. We further establish a monotonicity property showing that adding a locally superior expert cannot degrade the switching mechanism's performance. Experiments across cryptocurrency, stock, and foreign-exchange markets show that the proposed selector achieves the highest cumulative return and Sharpe ratio among the evaluated selection strategies in all three markets. In the stock market, for example, cumulative return increases from 26% for the best fixed expert to 34%, while the Sharpe ratio improves from 0.74 to 0.96. Ablation results confirm the importance of both retrieval and LLM reasoning, while experiments with different expert-pool sizes demonstrate the value of complementary expertise. Overall, the findings support retrieval-grounded expert switching as an effective approach to adaptive portfolio management in non-stationary financial environments.

</details>


### [174] [Physics-Guided Flow Matching for CT Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28256)
*Davide Evangelista*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Flow Matching作为CT图像重建的生成先验，在Mayo Clinic低剂量CT数据集上训练了256×256的Rectified Flow Matching模型，并提出两阶段训练策略缓解过拟合。实验表明Flow Matching方法在PSNR、SSIM和感知质量上优于扩散模型方法，且所需采样步数更少，并公开了模型和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在CT逆问题重建中虽表现优异，但存在随机采样过程长、推理轨迹耗时、噪声调度需精细调节等问题，尤其在高分辨率场景下计算效率和数值稳定性受限。作者旨在探索Flow Matching作为更稳定、高效的替代生成先验。

Method: 训练高分辨率Rectified Flow Matching模型（256×256胸片），采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段使用强解剖信息数据增强，第二阶段减少或去除增强以细化结构保真度。评估多种为Flow Matching设计的重建方法（Plug-and-Play Flow、FlowDPS、Flower、Flow-Priors/ICTM），并与扩散方法（DDRM、DPS、DiffPIR）对比。

Result: 在多种CT逆问题设置下，Flow Matching方法在PSNR、SSIM和感知质量上一致优于扩散方法，且所需采样步数更少。生成的图像具有高质量和解剖一致性。

Conclusion: Flow Matching是高分辨率CT重建中扩散模型的稳定、高效、有效替代方案，训练好的模型和代码已公开发布以促进可重复性和后续研究。

Abstract: Deep generative models have recently emerged as powerful priors for solving ill-posed inverse problems in CT, with diffusion-based approaches achieving state-of-the-art reconstruction performance. However, diffusion models typically rely on stochastic sampling procedures, long inference trajectories, and carefully tuned noise schedules, which can limit computational efficiency and numerical stability, especially at high spatial resolutions. In this work, we investigate Flow Matching as an alternative generative prior for CT reconstruction. We train a high-resolution Rectified Flow Matching model on 256x256 chest images from the Mayo Clinic Low-Dose CT dataset. To mitigate overfitting and limited anatomical variability, we employ a two-stage training strategy consisting of an initial phase with strong, anatomically informed data augmentation, followed by a fine-tuning phase with reduced or no augmentation to refine structural fidelity. The resulting model is capable of generating high-quality and anatomically coherent CT-like images, serving as a strong learned prior. We then evaluate multiple reconstruction methods specifically designed for Flow Matching models, including Plug-and-Play Flow, FlowDPS, Flower, and Flow-Priors (ICTM), and compare them against state-of-the-art diffusion-based reconstruction algorithms such as DDRM, DPS, and DiffPIR. Experimental results across several CT inverse problem settings show that Flow Matching-based approaches consistently outperform diffusion-based methods in terms of PSNR, SSIM, and perceptual quality, while requiring fewer sampling steps. Finally, we publicly release the trained Flow Matching model and accompanying code to facilitate reproducibility and future research. Overall, this work demonstrates that Flow Matching provides a stable, efficient, and effective alternative to diffusion models for high-resolution CT image reconstruction.

</details>


### [175] [Finding Where the Buck Stops: An Automated Failure Attribution-Based Reflection Framework for Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28264)
*Xiaoqing Wang,Keman Huang,Bin Liang,Hongyu Li,Xiaoyong Du,Wuqiong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DoCtOR框架，通过自动化失败归因识别关键错误代理和步骤，仅让该代理进行针对性反思，避免污染其他正常代理的记忆。在HotPotQA、ChartQAPro和Mind2Web上分别提升22%、26%和27%，优于Reflexion、Retroformer和COPPER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统自反思方法要求所有代理对所有失败进行反思，但实际失败往往源于单个关键错误代理（decisive error agent），强制其他正常工作的代理反思会污染其记忆并引入错误见解。

Method: DoCtOR框架包含三步：1) 通过自动化失败归因识别关键错误步骤和关键错误代理；2) 采用反事实推理生成修正后的关键错误步骤；3) 仅让关键错误代理进行针对性反思。同时采用PPO增强策略优化反思效果。

Result: 在HotPotQA、ChartQAPro和Mind2Web数据集上，DoCtOR相较于初始成功率分别提升22%、26%和27%，超越Reflexion、Retroformer和COPPER方法。此外验证了diagnose-then-correct范式的通用性，并证明在低资源场景下仅对关键错误步骤后的推理进行反思即可达到与全轨迹反思相当的质量。

Conclusion: 诊断后修正的反思范式能显著提升多智能体协作效率，聚焦关键错误代理进行针对性反思比全员泛化反思更有效，且在资源受限场景下具有良好适应性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by large language models have shown promise for complex tasks but suffer from high failure rates. Current self-reflection methods for MAS require all agents to reflect upon failure, overlooking a critical reality: failures typically stem from a specific agent leading the task astray, namely the decisive error agent, while others merely fulfill their regular duties. Forcing regular-behaving agents to reflect contaminates their memory with wrong insights. Hence, we propose DoCtOR (Diagnose-then-Correct PPO-enhanced Reflection), a novel reflection framework that enhances multi-agent collaboration. DoCtOR first identifies the decisive error step and decisive error agent through automated failure attribution, then employs counterfactual reasoning to generate a corrected decisive error step, and finally engages only the decisive error agent to produce targeted reflections. Experimental results show DoCtOR achieves 22%, 26%, and 27% improvements over initial success rates on HotPotQA, ChartQAPro, and Mind2Web datasets, outperforming Reflexion, Retroformer, and COPPER. We further establish the generalizability of our diagnose-then-correct paradigm and demonstrate that in low-resource settings, focusing reflection on reasoning steps after the decisive error step achieves comparable quality to reflecting on the complete failure trajectory.

</details>


### [176] [RECAST: Recent & Context-Aware Sampling for Test-Time Adaptation in Streaming Biosignals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28271)
*Yong-Yeon Jo,Junho Song,Joon-myoung Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RECAST，一种轻量级采样模块，用于流式生物信号的测试时自适应（TTA）。它从时间近因、上下文相似性和预测可靠性三个维度构建自适应批次，仅改变采样方式而不修改模型和训练目标，在血压数据集上显著提升估计精度和趋势追踪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式生物信号存在跨对象差异和随时间漂移的问题，人群训练的模型在长期监测中精度下降。测试时自适应（TTA）虽能实现在线个性化，但流式环境中"哪些样本应驱动每次更新"这一基本问题尚未解决：全量缓冲样本会引入无关片段干扰更新，仅用最新片段则导致噪声大、不稳定。最有用的样本应兼具近期性、与当前生理状态对齐以及足够的可靠性。

Method: RECAST从三个信号维度构建自适应批次：(1) 时间近因（temporal recency）——最近样本优先；(2) 上下文相似性（contextual similarity）——与当前生理状态对齐的样本；(3) 预测可靠性（predictive reliability）——模型可学习信任的样本。该模块仅替换TTA框架中的采样策略，不影响底层模型结构和训练目标，保持轻量、可即插即用。

Result: 在两个血压数据集上，RECAST相比基线和消融方法显著提升了估计精度和趋势跟踪能力。在常规基准测试中，各患者指标均有广泛改善；在急诊场景下，增益集中在最困难的患者群体。每患者增益在两个数据集上均具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: RECAST解决了流式TTA中的核心采样问题，通过近因、上下文和可靠性三重信号构建自适应批次，在几乎零额外开销（单GPU和CPU核心下每段仅增加亚秒级延迟）的情况下显著提升血压监测的长期精度和趋势追踪能力，是一种实用且有效的方案。

Abstract: Streaming biosignals vary across subjects and drift over time, so population-trained models lose accuracy during long-term monitoring. Test-time adaptation (TTA) enables online personalization by updating the model on incoming samples. But in a stream, a basic question is left open: \emph{which samples should drive each update?} Using all buffered samples blurs the update with irrelevant segments. Using only the latest segment makes the update noisy and unstable. The most useful samples are recent, aligned with the current physiological state, and reliable enough to learn from. We propose \textbf{RECAST} (REcent \& Context-Aware Sampling for TTA), a lightweight sampling module for buffered TTA frameworks. RECAST builds each adaptation batch from three signals: temporal recency, contextual similarity, and predictive reliability. It changes only which samples are used, leaving the model and the training objective unchanged. On two blood-pressure datasets, RECAST improves estimation accuracy and trend tracking over baselines and ablations. The per-patient gains are statistically significant on both datasets, with broad improvement on the regular benchmark and gains concentrated on the hardest patients in the emergency-department setting. RECAST stays practical, adding only sub-second latency per segment on a single GPU and CPU core.

</details>


### [177] [LoopArena: Benchmarking Models as Runtime Controllers for Loop Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28281)
*Yi Wang,Haopeng Zhang,Chengxiang Huang,Rui Dai,Kaikui Liu,Piotr Koniusz,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoopArena是一个用于评估"控制器"模型如何引导独立编码代理完成长时任务的基准测试，包含三种不同粒度的评估设置，在完整任务上最佳严格成功率为24.69%，同时推断成本平均降低64.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来循环工程（Loop Engineering）成为围绕编码代理组织开发工作的重要实践，但现有评估无法区分最终结果的好坏究竟来自循环本身的引导质量还是编码代理的执行能力。需要一个专门评估控制器引导能力的基准。

Method: 提出LoopArena基准：将被评估的模型设为"控制器"（Controller），每轮编码后接收结构化运行摘要，指导一个固定的"工作者"（Worker）编码代理下一步做什么或验证什么，或决定是否停止。通过三种互补设置进行评估：Type I使用执行验证的题目选择下一步循环合约（无需运行Worker）；Type II在任务切片上执行重复控制；Type III从原始状态评估完整任务的配对执行。

Result: 完整任务上最佳严格成功率为24.69%；各控制器对比中，估计推断成本平均降低64.4%；Type II在主要Core标准下与完整评估的排序高度一致（Spearman's ρ=0.9747）。

Conclusion: LoopArena填补了循环工程中控制器能力评估的空白，结果表明长视野循环控制仍有大量改进空间，同时Type II设置可作为低成本且可靠的评估替代方案。基准数据和评估代码已开源。

Abstract: Loop Engineering is emerging as a practice for organizing development work around coding agents. Instead of writing each prompt by hand, practitioners design loops that monitor progress, assign work, run checks, and decide what the agent should do next. Even with a capable coding agent, a loop may trust a stale progress note, skip needed verification, spend its budget in the wrong direction, or stop before the task is safe to submit. Yet the final outcome of one end-to-end run cannot tell whether success or failure reflects the loop's guidance or the coding agent's ability to carry out the task. We introduce LoopArena, a benchmark for evaluating how well one model can guide a separate coding agent through a long-running task. The model under evaluation is the \textbf{Controller}: after each coding round, it receives a structured summary of the run and instructs a separate, fixed coding agent, the \textbf{Worker}, on what to do or verify next, or decides whether to stop. LoopArena evaluates this ability in three complementary settings that differ in execution scope and cost. Type I scores next-step Loop Contract selection through execution-validated questions without running the Worker at evaluation time. Type II executes repeated control over a selected slice of a full task, while Type III evaluates the paired full task from its original state. On full tasks, the best observed Strict Success Rate is \textbf{24.69\%}, leaving substantial room for improvement in long-horizon loop control. Across Controllers, the paired reduction in estimated inference cost averages \textbf{64.4\%}, and Type II produces a similar ordering under the main Core criterion (Spearman's \(ρ=\textbf{0.9747}\)). We release the benchmark data and evaluation code at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/LoopArena .

</details>


### [178] [Memristive-Friendly Hadamard Reservoir Computing: Structured, Multiplier-Free Recurrences at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28295)
*Andrea Ceni,Gianluca Milano,Carlo Ricciardi,Claudio Gallicchio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用结构化正交算子（由符号对角矩阵、置换和快速Walsh-Hadamard变换组成）替代储层计算（RC）中的密集递归矩阵，该算子免乘法器、仅需O(N)参数和O(N log N)步运算，且不需要物化为矩阵。通过数学分析证明精确正交性可保证紧缩的回波状态条件和可预测的噪声响应，在多个基准测试和硬件平台上验证了其与密集正交储层相当的精度和显著的速度/内存优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储层计算（RC）围绕固定（未经训练）的递归层设计循环神经网络，是神经形态硬件的自然候选方案。然而，现有的忆阻器友好型储层虽然从忆阻器件动力学衍生出神经元动态，但仍依赖密集递归矩阵，这在物理实现上成本高昂。核心动机是用结构化算子替代密集矩阵，以降低硬件实现成本和计算复杂度。

Method: 将储层中的密集递归矩阵替换为由符号对角矩阵、置换矩阵和快速Walsh-Hadamard变换（FWHT）构建的结构化正交算子。该算子免乘法、仅需O(N)参数和O(N log N)每步运算，且无需物化为完整矩阵。将其应用于标准回波状态网络（ESN）和忆阻器友好型ESN中，每个单元仅使用一个二进制输入连接。通过数学分析证明精确正交性带来的回波状态条件和噪声响应性质，并进行了多种硬件平台测试和消融实验。

Result: 在二十个分类和七个回归基准上（储层规模最大达N=8192），结构化模型与密集正交储层性能相当，平均性能优于循环储层（cycle reservoir），且优势随规模增大而扩大。在三种硬件平台上，递归步骤比密集乘积快最多50倍，内存占用小10^4倍。此外还通过消融实验测量了噪声、量化、器件失配和离散故障下的响应。

Conclusion: 结构化正交算子能够在保持储层性能的同时，大幅降低计算复杂度和硬件实现成本，是面向神经形态硬件的高效储层设计方案。

Abstract: Reservoir Computing (RC) designs Recurrent Neural Networks around a fixed, i.e., untrained, recurrent layer, and is a natural candidate for neuromorphic hardware. Memristive-friendly reservoirs derive the neuron dynamics from memristive-device kinetics, but still rely on dense recurrent matrices, which are expensive to realize physically. In this paper, we replace the dense matrix with a structured orthogonal operator, built from sign diagonals, a permutation, and a fast Walsh-Hadamard transform. The operator is multiplier-free, requires $O(N)$ parameters and $O(N\log N)$ operations per step, and is never materialized as a matrix. We instantiate it in a standard and in a memristive-friendly Echo State Network, with one binary input connection per unit.
  Our mathematical analysis shows that exact orthogonality yields an echo state condition that is tight in the recurrent scaling, and a noise response that is predictable at design time. Moreover, the operator mixes the whole state in a single application. Experiments on twenty classification and seven regression benchmarks, at reservoir sizes up to $N = 8192$, show that the structured models match dense orthogonal reservoirs, and achieve better mean performance than the cycle reservoir by a margin that widens with size. Furthermore, we time the recurrent step on three hardware platforms, where it is up to $50\times$ faster than a dense product and $10^4\times$ smaller in memory. Finally, we ablate the operator and measure the response to noise, quantization, device mismatch and discrete faults.

</details>


### [179] [MAIL: Memory-driven, Adaptive, Incremental, and Literature-grounded Framework for Hypothesis Generation in Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28315)
*Mahdi Babaei,Xueshen Li,Yutao Kuang,Jolene P. Reid,Yu Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAIL框架，一种用于化学科学假设生成的记忆增强、自适应、增量式、基于文献的自动化方法，将假设生成建模为时间接地、记忆驱动的推理过程，在TOMATO-Chem和HN-NS数据集上取得了最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学文献体量快速增长为生成新颖假设提供了机会，但现有方法依赖静态灵感语料、预定义启发式或繁琐的人工参与流程，限制了可扩展性和新颖性，需要一种自动化的高质量假设生成方法。

Method: MAIL框架将假设生成形式化为时间接地、记忆驱动的推理过程。核心思想是让假设从一条不断演化、持续积累并重新解读先前知识的概念路径中涌现，通过记忆增强和自适应增量式学习来生成化学假设。

Result: 在TOMATO-Chem数据集和新构建的HN-NS（高新颖性nature/science挑战）数据集上评估，MAIL生成的假设在结构连贯性和机理合理性方面表现良好，取得了最高的MIOS和MPOS指标，更有效地恢复了历史目标假设的核心思想和方法要素，并且在科学质量的专家评估中得分最高。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明LLM具备自主探索化学领域并生成既创新又化学上合理的假设的潜力，MAIL框架为实现这一目标提供了有效的自动化途径。

Abstract: The ever-expanding volume of the chemical literature offers unprecedented opportunities to generate novel and impactful hypotheses. However, the bottleneck lies in efficiently navigating this vast knowledge base to formulate high-quality, experimentally meaningful insights.
  While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise for this task, existing methods often rely on static inspiration corpora, predefined heuristics, or laborious human-in-the-loop pipelines and decision-support frameworks that limit scalability and novelty. In this work, we propose an automated approach, a Memory-augmented, Adaptive, Incremental, and Literature-grounded (MAIL) framework for hypothesis generation in chemistry. Our MAIL method formulates hypothesis generation as a temporally grounded, memory-driven reasoning process, where hypotheses emerge from an evolving conceptual path that continuously accumulates and reinterprets prior knowledge. We evaluated the MAIL framework on a public TOMATO-Chem dataset and a newly curated and disseminated high-novelty nature/science challenge (HN-NS) dataset. Across both datasets, MAIL generates structurally coherent and mechanistically plausible hypotheses, achieves the highest MIOS and MPOS by more effectively recovering the central ideas and methodological elements of the historical target hypotheses, and obtains the highest overall expert-evaluation scores for scientific quality. These results demonstrate the potential of LLMs to autonomously explore chemical domains and generate hypotheses that are both innovative and chemically plausible.

</details>


### [180] [Real-Valued Hyperdimensional Sequence Representations with Hadamard Product Binding and Shift Equivariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28334)
*Kenny Schlegel,Dmitri A. Rachkovskij,Denis Kleyko,Amy Loutfi,Stefan Streif,Evgeny Osipov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三种基于随机傅里叶特征的真实值位置编码变体，以替代标准分数幂编码（FPE），在保留FPE相似性保持和移位等变性质的同时，支持Hadamard乘积绑定。其中Sinusoid变体提供显式代数移位算子，无需重新编码即可直接应用时间移位，且实验表明在时间序列分类上性能与标准FPE相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分数幂编码（FPE）主要针对循环卷积或复数乘法等绑定操作设计，与实数向量的Hadamard乘积绑定不兼容，限制了其在超维计算中的适用性。作者希望开发一种既能保留FPE理想性质又支持Hadamard积运算的实数位置编码方法。

Method: 受随机傅里叶特征启发，提出三种实数位置编码变体：（1）基于逆傅里叶变换的实数基线；（2）Sinusoid表示；（3）仅余弦表示。其中Sinusoid变体具备显式代数移位算子，可直接对向量编码的序列表示施加时间移位。

Result: 在时间序列分类数据集上，提出的实数表示性能与标准FPE相当，同时支持计算高效的Hadamard乘积绑定。Sinusoid变体在实数实现效率与精确移位等变变换之间取得了最优平衡。

Conclusion: 基于随机傅里叶特征设计的实数位置编码为超维计算提供了更灵活的替代方案，特别是Sinusoid变体在不损失性能的前提下，实现了精确移位等变和高效Hadamard绑定，具有实际应用的推广价值。

Abstract: Encoding temporal order is a fundamental requirement for sequence representations in Hyperdimensional Computing. Fractional Power Encoding provides similarity-preserving position vectors whose inner products approximate shift-invariant kernels, and it supports shift-equivariant transformations of encoded sequence representations. However, standard formulations of Fractional Power Encoding are primarily designed for binding operations such as circular convolution or complex-valued multiplication, which limits their compatibility with Hadamard product binding of real-valued vectors. This paper develops real-valued position encodings motivated by Random Fourier Features, aiming to retain the desirable properties of Fractional Power Encoding while supporting Hadamard-based operations. We propose three real-valued position-encoding variants: a real-valued baseline based on the inverse Fourier transform, and Sinusoid and Cosine-only representations derived from Random Fourier Features. Among them, the Sinusoid variant provides an explicit algebraic shift operator, allowing temporal shifts to be applied directly to the vector-encoded sequence representation without re-encoding the shifted sequence. Experiments on time-series classification datasets show that the proposed real-valued representations achieve performance comparable to standard Fractional Power Encoding while enabling computationally efficient Hadamard product binding. The Sinusoid variant offers the most favorable trade-off, combining efficient real-valued implementation with exact shift-equivariant transformations.

</details>


### [181] [AGENT-O: A Semantic Agent Card Framework for Interoperable and Governed Healthcare AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28345)
*Pengze Li,Cui Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGENT-O是一个模块化本体框架，定义了语义化Agent Card用于表示面向健康的AI智能体系统，并支持对科学出版物中报告完整性的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学出版物中AI智能体系统的报告往往不完整且缺乏结构化标准，研究者需要一个统一的语义框架来表示智能体系统并评估其报告完整性，以识别关键信息缺失。

Method: 开发OWL 2/RDF本体AGENT-O，涵盖运行时、模型、工作流、工具、临床使用、评估、来源、治理及报告评估等维度；通过本体清单、OWL-RL推理、三套SHACL套件、12个SPARQL能力查询、三个案例以及基于模型的报告完整性评估（279篇论文、五个维度）进行验证。

Result: 本体包含1,962个RDF三元组和1,922个Protege公理，252个活动类、198个活动对象属性和51个数据类型属性。所有SHACL套件在示例图上均通过，所有能力查询均返回预设证据，279篇论文均获评分。报告不完整最高的维度为运行时/架构（84.6%）、治理/安全（82.8%）和来源/可复现性（78.1%），而评估（25.8%）和基准流程对齐（29.8%）的报告更完整。

Conclusion: AGENT-O揭示了评估-规范差距：评估和基准流程的报告一致性高于运行时架构、治理和可复现性。该框架提供可复用的本体、语义Agent Card配置文件和报告完整性工作流，用于结构化报告和差距识别，但不评估智能体质量或部署就绪性。

Abstract: AGENT-O is a modular ontology framework that defines a semantic Agent Card for representing health-oriented AI agent systems and supports assessment of reporting completeness in scientific publications. AGENT-O was developed as an OWL 2/RDF ontology covering runtime, models, workflow, tools, clinical use, evaluation, provenance, governance, and reporting assessment. Evaluation included ontology inventory, OWL-RL reasoning, three SHACL suites, 12 SPARQL competency queries, three cases, and model-assisted reporting-completeness assessment of 279 papers across five dimensions. The ontology contained 1,962 RDF triples and 1,922 Protege axioms, with 252 active classes, 198 active object properties, and 51 datatype properties. All SHACL suites conformed on example graphs, all competency queries returned prespecified evidence, and all 279 papers were scored. Incomplete reporting was highest for runtime/architecture (84.6%), governance/safety (82.8%), and provenance/reproducibility (78.1%), compared with evaluation (25.8%) and benchmark-process alignment (29.8%). AGENT-O supported semantic Agent Card representation and reporting assessment while revealing an evaluation-specification gap: evaluation and benchmark procedures were reported more consistently than runtime architecture, governance, and reproducibility. AGENT-O provides a reusable ontology, semantic Agent Card profile, and reporting-completeness workflow for structured reporting and gap identification, but does not assess agent quality or deployment readiness.

</details>


### [182] [Propagating construction-time knowledge quality into medical question answering: A framework grounded in clinical guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28360)
*Jie Hu,Junjie Wang,Shan Lu,Yifang Hu,Gong Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种质量感知框架，将知识图谱构建时的三元组质量信号（结构符合度SchemaConf与证据支持度EvidScore）融合为持久化的图属性，在检索阶段通过质量加权子图选择和分层证据提示，弥合了KG构建质量控制与推理阶段证据使用之间的断层，实验表明该方法能显著降低问答中的知识遗漏和冲突输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型从临床指南构建知识图谱时，提取的三元组在结构有效性和证据支持度上存在差异，但现有的图增强问答系统在检索时只优化查询相关性，未复用构建阶段产生的质量信息，导致构建时质量控制与推理时证据使用脱节。

Method: 提出质量感知框架：构建SchemaConf（结构符合度）和EvidScore（证据支持度）两个互补维度，融合为每个三元组的质量信号Q(t)；将Q(t)和质量分层作为图属性保留，而非仅用于过滤；在检索时进行质量加权子图检索，并基于质量层级进行条件化证据提示，同时保留passage级别的溯源信息。

Result: 在中文糖尿病临床指南实验上：融合的Q(t)在三元组质量判别上优于单一维度（AUC 0.748 vs EvidScore 0.703, SchemaConf 0.645）；问答中知识遗漏从16.3%降至5.3%，冲突输出从16.3%降至2.7%，证据接地精度达81.6%且无效引用近乎为零；盲法临床医师评分中完整框架为4.68分（无检索4.21分，接近oracle条件的4.80分）；跨生成器实验趋势一致。

Conclusion: 构建时三元组质量可以作为下游证据选择与呈现的持久信号，且质量信号的有效性具有分布依赖性；将质量控制从过滤工具转变为图属性传播机制，能够显著提升临床指南问答系统的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models have facilitated knowledge graph (KG) construction from clinical guidelines, but extracted triples vary in structural validity and evidential support. Meanwhile, graph-augmented question answering (QA) systems typically optimize query relevance during retrieval, with limited reuse of quality information produced during KG construction. This creates a disconnect between construction-time quality control and inference-time evidence use. We investigate whether construction-time triple quality can serve as a persistent signal for downstream evidence selection and presentation. We propose a quality-aware framework that models structural conformance (SchemaConf) and evidential support (EvidScore) as complementary dimensions and fuses them into a per-triple quality signal, Q(t). Rather than using quality solely for filtering, the framework retains Q(t) and derived quality tiers as graph attributes and propagates them into quality-weighted subgraph retrieval and tier-conditioned evidence prompting, while preserving passage-level provenance. Experiments on Chinese diabetes clinical guidelines show that the utility of the quality signal is distribution dependent. Under cross-version and cross-model shift, the fused Q(t) provides stronger triple-quality discrimination than either component alone (AUC 0.748 vs. 0.703 for EvidScore and 0.645 for SchemaConf). In guideline-grounded QA, propagating construction-time quality reduces required-knowledge omission from 16.3% to 5.3% and conflicting outputs from 16.3% to 2.7%, with an evidence-grounded precision of 81.6% and near-zero invalid citations. Blinded clinician ratings favor the full framework over no retrieval (4.68 vs. 4.21 on a five-point scale) and approach the oracle condition (4.80), while cross-generator experiments show consistent trends.

</details>


### [183] [GRACE:Gradient-guided Coreset Selection for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28361)
*Praveen Bushipaka,Andrea D'Angelo,Lucia Passaro,Tommaso Cucinotta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE，一种基于梯度引导的coreset选择方法，用于大语言模型机器遗忘中的数据选择问题。该方法从少量种子示例推导遗忘方向，利用非负正交匹配追踪选择遗忘coreset，并在投影掉遗忘方向后通过聚类正交匹配追踪选择保留示例，在多种场景下提升了模型效用同时保持遗忘质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型机器遗忘方法通常假设预先指定遗忘集和保留集，但在实际应用中，用户请求往往只提供少量不良行为的示例，需要从未标注的异质语料中推断遗忘集和保留集，这一数据选择问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: GRACE包含两阶段：首先从触发不良行为的种子示例中计算遗忘梯度方向，通过非负正交匹配追踪（NOMP）选择紧凑的遗忘coreset，使所选样本的梯度近似该方向；然后在投影掉遗忘方向后的梯度空间中，应用聚类正交匹配追踪（COMP）选择保留示例，以保持模型效用。

Result: 在两种目标领域、两个模型家族和四种遗忘算法上，GRACE在保持可比遗忘质量的同时提升了模型效用，且相较于先前的基于梯度的选择方法表现出持续一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: GRACE有效解决了LLM遗忘中从异质语料推断遗忘集和保留集的数据选择问题，验证了梯度引导的coreset选择在机器遗忘任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning methods for Large Language Models typically assume pre-specified forget and retain sets. In realistic settings, however, requests may provide only a few examples of undesired behavior, requiring forget and retain sets to be inferred from heterogeneous corpora. We study this data-selection problem and propose GRACE , a gradient-guided coreset selection method that constructs both forget and retain sets for LLM unlearning. GRACE first computes a forget direction from seed examples that elicit the undesired behavior, then selects a compact forget coreset whose gradients approximate this direction using non-negative orthogonal matching pursuit. To preserve model utility, it selects retain examples after projecting out the forget direction and applying clustered orthogonal matching pursuit in the remaining gradient space. Across two target domains, two model families, and four unlearning algorithms, GRACE improves model utility while maintaining comparable forget quality, with particularly consistent gains over prior gradient-based selection methods.

</details>


### [184] [EvoUndo: Recoverability-Constrained Self-Evolution for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28363)
*Tanmay Sah,Dolly Sah,Harshul Jain,Tanya Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoUndo框架，用于表示、合成、诊断和独立验证LLM代理自我修改的可恢复性。在600个未见过的单次自我进化任务中，发现197个能力提升但无法通过可恢复性验证的突变。通过扩展恢复演算和精确状态地址接地干预，将恢复率从传统的0/197提升到191/197，表明可靠的代理自我进化需要协同设计验证、状态接地、见证语义和恢复语言表达力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在运行时修改自身提示、工具和执行框架以实现自我进化，但成功的突变可能留下无法安全逆转的持久影响。需要一种系统方法来验证模型生成的自我修改是否可恢复。

Method: 提出EvoUndo框架，包含四个关键组件：表示（recovery language L0及扩展演算）、合成、诊断和独立验证。使用协议锁定的2x2接地-表达力干预实验来分离状态地址接地和恢复语言表达力两个瓶颈。

Result: 在600个任务中识别出197个失败的可恢复性验证。原始恢复表示下传统修复策略恢复0/197；扩展恢复演算将确定性oracle恢复提升至191/197。精确状态地址接地将原始语言的恢复率从0/48提升到38/48（79.2%），扩展语言在oracle定义的S1层实现142/143（99.3%）的恢复率。

Conclusion: 可靠的代理自我进化需要协同设计验证、状态接地、见证语义和恢复语言表达力，而非仅依赖迭代提示。同时发现某些负面交互是模型依赖的（如gpt-oss-120b上的接地与表达力负交互在Qwen3.8-27B上未复现）。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly modify their own prompts, tools, middleware, resources, and execution harnesses at runtime. Such self-evolution can improve capability, but a successful mutation may leave persistent effects that cannot be safely reversed in states different from the one in which it was created. We introduce EvoUndo, a framework for representing, synthesizing, diagnosing, and independently verifying recoverability of model-generated self-modifications across counterfactual states. Across 600 unseen one-shot self-evolution tasks, we identify 197 capability-improving mutations that fail recoverability verification. Under the original recovery representation, conventional repair strategies recover 0/197 of these natural failures. Deterministic oracle analysis recovers 48/197 under the original recovery language L0, while the extended recovery calculus increases empirical oracle recovery to 191/197. A protocol-locked 2x2 grounding-by-expressivity intervention then separates two bottlenecks: exact state-address grounding increases successful recovery from 0/48 to 38/48 (79.2%) when the original language is sufficient, while extending the recovery language enables recovery on 142/143 (99.3%) failures in the oracle-defined S1 stratum. On the primary gpt-oss-120b backbone, adding exact-address diagnostics to the richer language reduces recovery to 133/143 (93.0%); a Qwen3.8-27B replication preserves the grounding and expressivity effects but not this negative interaction, indicating that the latter is model-dependent. These results indicate that reliable agent self-evolution requires co-designing verification, state grounding, witness semantics, and recovery-language expressivity rather than relying on iterative prompting alone.

</details>


### [185] [MAP: A Benchmark on Multimodal Accessibility Planning for Real World Places](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28384)
*Jason Armitage,Ioannis Tsochantaridis,Linda Mazzone,Chuqiao Yan,Srini Narayanan,Sarah Ebling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MAP，这是首个用于评估多模态AI系统作为无障碍需求用户出行规划助手的基准测试，包含两类新评估任务：无障碍规划声明验证和视觉证据检索，并支持随时间变化的信息更新与人工/自动评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中，有行动障碍等无障碍需求的用户在规划出行时面临诸多困难，现有AI助手缺乏针对这一场景的系统性评估基准，难以衡量多模态AI系统在验证、推荐符合无障碍要求场所方面的能力。

Method: MAP基准包含两个新颖的评估任务：1）无障碍规划声明验证——评估系统对场所信息及无障碍声明的支持性判断，以及识别满足要求的场所；2）无障碍规划视觉证据检索——检验多模态AI系统能否选取与场所及无障碍特征相关的视觉证据。该方法支持按计划时间刷新评估与真实标注数据以适应信息变化，并采用自动评分加人工评分的混合评估方式。

Result: 论文提出了包含两项新评估任务的MAP基准，并设计了支持信息动态变化的可刷新评估流程，结合自动与人工评分机制以保障评估可靠性与可比性。

Conclusion: MAP填补了无障碍出行场景下多模态AI系统评估的空白，通过声明验证与视觉证据检索两大评估维度，为后续研究提供了可扩展、可更新的基准框架，推动AI系统更好服务有无障碍需求的用户。

Abstract: We introduce MAP, the first benchmark to evaluate multimodal AI systems as assistants for users with accessibility requirements when planning visits to places in the real world. In our evaluation, systems are presented with requests to verify or recommend a point of interest meeting an accessibility requirement. MAP contains two novel assessments: Claim verification for accessibility planning assesses if information on places and stated accessibility features is supported and identifies places that satisfy requested accessibility features. Visual evidence retrieval for accessibility planning checks if a multimodal AI system can select visual evidence for the requested place and accessibility feature. Our methodology supports comparison of AI systems in a setting where place information and accessibility information can change over time by evaluating systems and refreshing ground truth data at scheduled times. The benchmark is based on automatic rating and human rating for a proportion of responses.

</details>


### [186] [Timing-Aware Repurchase Prediction for Web-Scale E-Commerce: Survival Models for Multi-Surface Grocery Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28393)
*Akshay Kekuda,Shreeranjani Srirangamsridharan,Ishan Bhatt,Yanan Cao,Sinduja Subramaniam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kaushiki Nag,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用生存分析模型（特别是AFT模型族）替代电商场景中按时间窗口分别训练的二元回购预测分类器，直接预测复购时间。基于大型杂货电商平台数百万客户和30余种消融配置，发现边际风险略微递减（k≈0.9），单一AFT模型可替代三个逐水平二元分类器且性能相当、树数量减少约3倍。此外提出4参数参数化校准方法将生存CDF映射为逐水平概率，实现零跨水平单调性违规，并揭示排名与校准之间可权衡的选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商复购推荐将问题建模为二元分类（"客户是否会在W天内购买"），需要为每个预测时间窗口单独训练模型，计算开销大且无法统一建模时间信息。作者希望用生存模型直接预测复购时间，用一个模型替代多个分类器，在保持性能的同时提升效率。

Method: 使用加速失效时间（AFT）生存模型族（包括Weibull、Log-Normal等），在大规模杂货电商数据上进行超过30种消融配置的实验。进行边缘与条件风险分析、特征重要性评估；提出4参数参数化校准方法将原始生存CDF映射为逐时间窗口概率；比较不同AFT分布（如Exponential与Log-Normal）在校准误差与排名指标上的表现差异。

Result: （1）边缘风险略递减（k≈0.9），与直觉相悖；Log-Normal边缘拟合最佳（R²=0.998）且排名最优，Weibull条件残差拟合最佳。（2）单个AFT模型可替代三个逐水平二元分类器，性能持平或超越，树数量减少约3倍；特征重要性在生存目标下重排，渠道节奏与近期性特征上升、聚合频率下降。（3）4参数校准实现零跨水平单调性违规；Exponential AFT校准误差（ECE≈1e-4）比Log-Normal低约10倍，排名指标相对差仅0.3%。

Conclusion: 在单一AFT家族内存在原理性的校准-排名权衡：概率消费型场景采用Exponential AFT以获得更优校准，纯排名场景采用Log-Normal以获更优排序。生存模型是电商复购推荐中更高效且灵活的替代方案。

Abstract: Repurchase recommenders in e-commerce are commonly framed as a binary question asking "will this customer buy this item within W days", a formulation that requires a separately trained model for every horizon of interest. We replace this stack with survival models that predict time-to-repurchase directly, and evaluate them on millions of customers from a major grocery e-commerce platform across more than thirty ablation configurations. Our study makes three contributions. First, an empirical hazard analysis reveals a slightly decreasing marginal hazard (k ~ 0.9), differing from the common intuition that grocery items become more likely to be repurchased the longer since the last purchase (increasing hazard, k > 1). Log-Normal achieves the best marginal fit (R^2 = 0.998) and the best ranking, despite Weibull providing the best conditional residual fit, revealing an apparent discrepancy we analyze in detail. Second, a single Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model replaces three per-horizon binary classifiers, matching or exceeding each at its own horizon while using roughly 3x fewer total trees. Feature importance reshuffles under the survival objective: channel-cadence and recency signals rise while aggregate frequency counts fall. Third, a 4-parameter parametric calibration maps raw survival CDFs to per-horizon probabilities with zero cross-horizon monotonicity violations. Calibration quality varies by an order of magnitude across the AFT family: Exponential AFT (Weibull k=1) achieves expected calibration error (ECE) ~1e-4, roughly 10x lower than Log-Normal, while ranking metrics agree within 0.3% relative. We adopt Exponential AFT for probability-consuming surfaces and Log-Normal for pure ranking, exposing a principled calibration-ranking trade-off within a single AFT family.

</details>


### [187] [RetailAgent: Structured Adverse Timing in Self-Conditioned Multimodal LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28399)
*Yupeng Zhang,Liuyuan Jiang,Hongyi Huang,Bingheng Li,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过RetailAgent实验框架研究LLMagent在金融交易中的方向性结构，发现其行为具有持续的负向择时特征（持有期间收益低于空仓期间），且这种模式在多种条件下稳定存在，表明LLM的序列化金融决策存在可预测的方向性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融市场中，对价格变动做出系统性反应的序列化策略可能被其他市场参与者预测。本文旨在探究LLM agent是否表现出这种方向性结构，从而理解LLM驱动的交易策略的可预测性及其对市场的影响。

Method: 构建RetailAgent实验框架：LLM观察匿名的日内股票价格历史及允许状态，在后续区间收益揭示前反复选择多头（持有）或空仓（观望）。通过暴露匹配度量（exposure-matched measure），在同一条股票的日内路径上比较多头与空仓区间的收益，并剔除多头决策的整体比例影响。实验还考察了改变模态、时间跨度、状态和模型家族的效果，并通过打乱保存的动作序列以及引入自撰记忆来验证效应的来源。

Result: 研究发现：（1）LLM agent表现出持续的负向择时，即在所有模态、时间跨度、状态和模型家族下均一致；（2）打乱动作序列会显著削弱该效应，表明动作与后续收益之间的对齐是负分数的驱动因素；（3）引入自撰记忆会进一步增强策略的持续性；（4）在同时使用两种动作的股票日上，负向择时更为明显。

Conclusion: LLM的序列化金融决策中存在稳定且可恢复的方向性结构，这为研究其他市场参与者如何应对可预测策略提供了行为信号，对理解LLM驱动的交易行为及其市场影响具有重要意义。

Abstract: In financial markets, a sequential policy that reacts systematically to price movements may become predictable to other market participants. This paper studies whether large language model (LLM) agents exhibit such directional structure through RetailAgent, an experimental framework in which an LLM observes anonymized intraday equity price histories and permitted state, then repeatedly chooses long (hold the stock) or flat (stay out) before the subsequent interval return is revealed. We compare returns during long and flat intervals along the same stock's intraday path after removing the overall fraction of long decisions. This exposure-matched measure reveals persistent negative timing across modality, horizon, state, and model family. Shuffling saved action sequences substantially attenuates the effect, showing that alignment between actions and subsequent returns drives the negative score. Feeding self-authored memories into decisions further increases policy persistence, while timing becomes more negative among stock-days on which the agent uses both actions. These results reveal stable, recoverable directional structure in sequential LLM financial decisions and a behavioral signal for studying how another participant could respond to a predictable policy.

</details>


### [188] [VERA-8B: Evidence-Grounded Audit Risk Reasoning from SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28402)
*Menghan Liu,Elynn Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VERA-8B，一个端到端审计推理系统，通过统一SFT和GRPO实现基于证据的审计风险预测，引入弃权和不确定性限定来处理不完整证据案例，并设计AuditBridge将原始文件转换为可验证记录和审阅报告，在预执法审计预测任务中超越所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准金融语言模型优先考虑流畅性而非证据支持，可能产生看似合理但含糊的答案，存在"基础差距"（grounding gap），使其不适合审计工作。审计应用要求判断必须有合理证据支持，因此需要构建专门面向证据约束的审计推理系统。

Method: 1) 首次将SFT（监督微调）与GRPO（群组相对策略优化）统一在同一证据标准下进行审计推理；2) 引入弃权机制和不确定性限定，对不确定或证据不完整的案例进行延迟处理；3) 设计AuditBridge组件，将原始申报文件转换为经过验证的记录，再生成审阅者就绪的报告，弥合金融与计算的鸿沟。

Result: VERA-8B在预执法审计预测任务中超越所有评估的基线模型，实现了更强的证据基础推理性能，并能生成可审计、可供审阅者直接使用的输出。

Conclusion: VERA-8B通过统一SFT与GRPO、引入弃权机制和AuditBridge管道，成功构建了第一个面向预执法审计预测的证据基础推理系统，产出的结果适合实际审计工作使用。

Abstract: Across audit applications, judgments must be supported by reasonable evidence. However, standard financial language models prioritize fluency over evidence. They are built for general financial reasoning and may produce plausible but ambiguous answers, creating a grounding gap that makes them unsuitable for audit work. We address this gap with VERA-8B, a new end-to-end audit reasoning system that identifies audit risks before enforcement actions occur. Constructing such a model raises several challenges, as no prior machine learning work targets pre-enforcement audit prediction. To our knowledge, we are the first to unify SFT and GRPO for evidence-grounded audit reasoning under one evidence standard, achieving performance that surpasses all evaluated baselines. Because auditing cannot tolerate unsupported claims, we introduce abstention and uncertainty qualification to defer uncertain or evidence-incomplete cases. Finally, we design an AuditBridge to ground model reasoning for practical audit work. It transforms raw filings into verified records and then into reviewer-ready reports, bridging finance and computation with broad generality. Together, these components produce auditable, review-ready outputs suitable for practical audit work.

</details>


### [189] [Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards: Symbolic Backpropagation for Post-Training LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28421)
*Vishvesh Bhat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PLVR（Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards）方法，将推理能力从模型权重中移出，以显式程序的形式放置在模型外部，通过符号反向传播学习程序，在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上以30B参数模型超越RL方法27.8分，并超越高一个数量级的尖端模型13.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统后训练方法（SFT和RL）将推理能力植入模型权重中，导致推理过程不可检查、不可分步验证、不可迁移到其他模型。对于中间步骤可验证的任务，应将推理放在模型外部作为显式程序。

Method: PLVR通过符号反向传播学习程序：每层程序携带类型化本体，在输出端与真值计算损失，并通过类型推断在原始签名上向后传播所需的输入本体——这是链式法则的类比，其中信用分配是推导而非估计。与RLVR验证终端结果不同，PLVR的奖励是每个步骤的合约判定，密集覆盖程序结构。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B基础模型配PLVR在匹配预算下平均超越RL 27.8分，超越大一个数量级的尖端模型13.6分。一个原始库服务于两个基准测试，新任务的边际成本仅为100个程序搜索示例。用均匀采样替换损失引导搜索后，中位数程序从65.6降至17.5，证明优势来源于反向传播而非类型系统。

Conclusion: 推理应放置在基础模型权重之外，以由确定性和神经原语组成的显式程序形式存在。PLVR的优势源于符号反向传播带来的高效信用分配，且原始库可复用，新任务边际成本极低。

Abstract: Post training a language model to reason means updating its weights. Supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning both place the acquired capability inside the model where it cannot be inspected cannot be checked step by step and cannot be moved to another model. We argue that for tasks whose intermediate steps admit verification, reasoning is better placed outside the base models weights as an explicit program composed from deterministic and neural primitives. We introduce PLVR (Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards): a post training method that learns such programs directly from input-output examples. Its mechanism is symbolic backpropagation: each program layer carries a typed ontology a loss is computed at the output against ground truth and required input ontologies are propagated backward by type inference over primitive signatures: an analogue of the chain rule in which credit assignment is a derivation rather than an estimate. Where RLVR verifies a terminal outcome, PLVRs reward is a per step contract verdict dense over program structure. On LiveCodeBench v6 and Tau2Bench, 30B base models with PLVR outperform RL at matched budget by 27.8 points on average and frontier models an order of magnitude larger by 13.6 points. A single primitive library serves two benchmarks, so the marginal cost of a new task is 100 examples of program search and no new finetuning data. Replacing the loss guided search with uniform sampling over the same type admissible space at equal budget collapses the median program from 65.6 to 17.5, identifying the backward pass rather than the type system as the source of the advantage. We release the symbolic backpropagation library and a conformance checker so the method can be applied to primitive libraries other than our own.

</details>


### [190] [Prove2Me: An Open Collaborative Platform for Scaling Math Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28433)
*Shuze Chen,Kunal Marwaha,Xiaoyang Lu,Henry Yuen,Tianyi Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Prove2Me，一个开放协作的数学形式化平台，允许用户发布形式化"任务"，AI智能体可协作完成Lean 4形式化证明，将数学形式化转变为可扩展的众包努力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lean 4等证明助手承诺了形式化验证数学的范式，但大规模形式化项目面临入门门槛高（需要形式化验证和数学双重专业知识）和编写形式化证明耗时长的障碍。尽管AI编码智能体已显著降低这些门槛，但仍需要一个支持人类和AI智能体大规模协作的平台。

Method: Prove2Me设计了一套支持大规模协作的机制和专业化的工具框架（harness），使得多个AI智能体可以彼此构建工作成果、自由复用已有结果，用户通过自然语言启动形式化"任务"，智能体协作贡献形式化证明以完成任务。

Result: Prove2Me平台已上线（https://prove2.me），旨在将数学形式化转变为可扩展的、面向任何拥有智能体的用户的众包努力。

Conclusion: Prove2Me为人类与AI智能体的互联网规模数学协作提供了可能，通过开放的协作机制和机器可验证的正确性，降低了形式化数学的入门门槛，使任何人只要有智能体即可参与数学形式化工作。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean 4 promise the paradigm of formally verified mathematics, but large-scale formalization projects have faced major barriers to entry, including the need for expertise in formal verification (as well as the underlying mathematics) and the significant time required for writing formal proofs. AI coding agents have dramatically reduced these barriers; human users can now use natural language to prompt agents to write complex proofs in Lean. This opens up the intriguing possibility of internet-scale mathematical collaboration involving both humans and AI agents, where correctness is machine-checked.
  To realize this possibility, we introduce Prove2Me (https://prove2.me), an open collaborative platform for formalizing mathematics. Users launch formalization "missions", to which AI agents contribute formal proofs toward completion. We designed mechanisms and a specialized harness in Prove2Me that enable large-scale collaboration so that agents can build on one another's work and freely reuse existing results. In doing so, Prove2Me aims to turn math formalization into a scalable, crowd-sourced effort open to anyone with an agent.

</details>


### [191] [Learning to Use Tools: Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28447)
*Minghui Xu,Zi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在Countdown数学推理任务中集成计算器工具的效果，发现工具集成可将pass@1从35.8%提升到66.0%，其中Tool-DAPO方法表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要可靠计算和验证的任务中常出现算术错误，本文旨在通过集成计算器工具来改进数学模型推理能力，减少计算错误。

Method: 首先分析推理失败原因，发现计算错误占比重大；然后构建监督微调数据集教会模型工具使用模式及输出解释；在工具格式化策略基础上应用多种强化学习方法（RLOO、RLOO++、GRPO、DAPO），使用可自动验证的最终答案奖励，并构建1024题的held-out基准进行评估。

Result: 计算器工具集成一致提升SFT和RL基线约10个百分点；Tool-DAPO表现最佳，pass@1从35.8%提升至66.0%；RL即使在仅有最终答案奖励的情况下也能促进更有效的工具使用。

Conclusion: 工具集成能减少算术和验证错误，而强化学习能提高正确推理轨迹的概率，二者结合可显著提升LLM的数学推理能力。

Abstract: Current large language models (LLMs) increasingly benefit from external tool integration, especially for tasks requiring reliable computation and verification. Motivated by this, we study calculator tool calling for improving mathematical reasoning on the Countdown task. We first analyze reasoning failures and find that calculation errors account for a substantial portion of incorrect responses. We then construct supervised fine-tuning datasets to teach the model useful tool-use patterns and how to interpret returned outputs. Building on this tool-formatted policy, we apply several on-policy reinforcement learning methods, including RLOO, RLOO++, GRPO, and DAPO, using automatically verifiable final-answer rewards. To enable a more reliable evaluation, we construct a fresh 1,024-problem held-out Countdown benchmark with no exact overlap with the training data. Our results show that calculator tool integration consistently improves both SFT and RL baselines, yielding roughly 10 percentage-point gains across pass@k. Among the RL methods, Tool-DAPO achieves the strongest performance, improving pass@1 from 35.8% for Tool-SFT to 66.0%. Further analysis shows that RL encourages more effective tool use even when only final-answer rewards are provided. These findings suggest that tool integration reduces arithmetic and verification errors, while RL increases the probability of correct reasoning traces.

</details>


### [192] [COVER: Identifiable Evaluation of Coalition Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28475)
*Raghul Sugumar,Amrit Gopinath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COVER评估合同，通过固定信息边界、下游栈G和有限合法团队族，在生成结果前定义评估框架，以识别多智能体系统路由的真实效果而非端到端准确率差距。实验表明COVER能暴露选择余量但不制造路由胜利，是一种可审计的测量方法而非通用优越性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统更换团队时，消息和最终答案同时改变，端到端准确率差距无法单独识别路由效应，需要一种能隔离路由贡献的评估方法。

Method: COVER定义了一个评估合同：预先固定公共信息边界、下游栈G和有限合法团队族。完整覆盖可识别有限基准的精确oracle遗憾。对任意有限冻结策略集合，执行其不同选定团队的并集是最小无假设支持，能成对对比策略但无法用于绝对oracle遗憾。通过源ID分离拆分的受控表格进行测试，并采用前瞻性指定对照和回顾性对照。

Result: MuSiQue-12上预指定正对照将遗憾从0.532降至0.402，公共接口对照达0.424对0.554但属回顾性。HotpotQA-4上预指定公共直接评分器将遗憾从0.313降至0.110。固定栈Llama执行中，验证路由遗憾改善0.190，而原始答案增益仅0.010且区间跨零。五族ToolSandbox变体迁移验证在224/224有效行上评估16个声明团队，声明族oracle达0.768，前瞻冻结路由器仅0.637（遗憾0.131），未达预声明0.10标准。

Conclusion: COVER暴露了选择余量但未制造路由胜利，交叉栈诊断表明绝对分数依赖G但未发现路由-终局器交互。COVER是可审计的测量方法论，并非栈不变或通用智能体路由优越性的声明。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system changes its team, it also changes the messages and final answer it produces, so an end-to-end accuracy gap does not by itself identify a routing effect. We introduce method, an evaluation contract that fixes a public information boundary, downstream stack G, and finite legal team family before outcomes are generated. Complete coverage identifies exact finite-benchmark oracle regret conditional on that stack. For any finite collection of frozen policies, executing the union of their distinct selected teams is the minimal assumption-free support for every pairwise policy contrast, though not for absolute oracle regret. Two controlled tables with source-ID-disjoint splits test the instrument. On MuSiQue-12, a pre-specified privileged positive control improves regret from 0.532 to 0.402; a later public-interface control reaches 0.424 versus 0.554 but is retrospective. On HotpotQA-4, a pre-specified public direct scorer improves regret from 0.313 to 0.110. In fixed-stack Llama execution, verified route regret improves by 0.190, while the raw-answer gain is 0.010 with an interval crossing zero. A five-family ToolSandbox variant-shift validation exhaustively evaluates 16 declared teams on 14 untouched task variants (224/224 valid rows): the declared-family oracle reaches 0.768 safe-evidence completion, while the prospectively frozen router gets 0.637 (regret 0.131), failing the predeclared 0.10 criterion. A later retrospective comparator reaches 0.655, matching all-workers with 4.57 versus 5.00 workers on average. Thus COVER exposes selection headroom without manufacturing a routing win. A crossed-stack diagnostic shows absolute scores depend on G but finds no detectable router-by-finalizer interaction. COVER is an auditable measurement methodology, not a claim of stack-invariant or universal agent-routing superiority.

</details>


### [193] [Training Communication-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Language Models with Layer Re-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28511)
*Simeng Sun,Roger Waleffe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种通信高效的MoE模型（CE-MoE），通过异构层模式将token混合和通道混合解耦，将专家容量集中在少数路由MoE层中，在匹配验证损失和下游基准的同时显著降低训练成本（31.5B规模下节省33.3% GPU小时）并提升推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专家并行训练MoE语言模型时，all-to-all token分发和组合集合通信会消耗大量端到端训练时间，传统模型在每次token混合层之后都穿插MoE层，导致通信开销过大。

Method: 采用异构层模式解耦token混合与通道混合深度，将专家能力集中在少数被路由的MoE层中，同时通过增加额外的token混合层和密集FFN层来保持模型深度。在2B到31.5B参数规模下进行缩放实验。

Result: 在匹配总参数量和激活参数量条件下，CE-MoE模型在全尺度上一致降低训练成本且匹配验证损失和下游基准。31.5B规模下使用33.3%更少的GPU小时，同时提升平均下游得分和推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: CE-MoE通过减少路由MoE层数量、降低通信开销，实现了在不牺牲模型质量的前提下更高效的MoE训练，可作为大规模MoE训练的实用替代方案。

Abstract: When training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models with expert parallelism, all-to-all token dispatch and combine collectives can consume a substantial fraction of end-to-end training time. In this work, we study communication-efficient MoE models (CE-MoE), in which we adopt a heterogeneous layer pattern that decouples token-mixing and channel-mixing depth. Compared to conventional models which interleave MoE layers after each token-mixing layer (e.g., attention, Mamba-2), CE-MoE models concentrate expert capacity in a select few routed MoE layers, while maintaining depth by adding additional token-mixing and dense-FFN layers. Across a scaling ladder from 2B to 31.5B total parameters, under matched total and activated parameters, CE-MoE models consistently reduce training cost while matching validation loss and downstream benchmarks with full-MoE baselines. At the 31.5B scale, CE-MoE uses 33.3\% fewer GPU-hours while improving average downstream score and inference throughput.

</details>


### [194] [InstructMesh: Selective Refinement of Generative 3D Models for Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28534)
*Faraz Faruqi,Ahmed Katary,Demircan Tas,Theresa Hradilak,Ning Zhang,Jiaji Li,Fabian Manhardt,Martin Nisser,Vrushank Phadnis,Ruofei Du,Federico Tombari,Megan Hofmann,Stefanie Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍InstructMesh，一个交互式后生成精修工具，允许用户通过区域选择和定向操作（如开孔、封孔、调整局部厚度）来修复生成式3D模型中的几何缺陷，支持自然语言提示和滑动条控制，面向非专业用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式AI 3D建模工具优先考虑视觉可信度而非几何精度，生成的模型在使用（如制造）后往往存在缺陷。用户缺乏简单有效的工具来修复这些几何问题，特别是非专业用户。

Method: 论文首先分析了先进生成工具输出中常见的制造相关失败模式，然后设计并实现了InstructMesh工具，该工具直接在中间潜在表示上操作，支持区域选择和定向编辑操作，并提供自然语言提示与滑动条控制的混合界面。

Result: 通过两项用户研究，证明新手用户能够使用InstructMesh识别并执行与制造相关的修复操作，同时发现用户倾向于混合使用滑动条控制和自然语言输入的界面。

Conclusion: InstructMesh提供了一种无需专业建模技能即可对生成式3D模型进行几何修正的有效方案，混合交互界面是提升用户体验的关键设计方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI allow users to create 3D models from text or images. However, these models prioritize visual plausibility over geometric accuracy, often generating results with flaws that compromise their intended use post-fabrication. We present InstructMesh, an interactive post-generation refinement tool that enables selective repair of generative 3D models through region selection and targeted operations, such as opening or sealing voids, or adjusting local thickness. Users can invoke edit operations via natural language prompts or slider controls. By operating directly on the intermediate latent representation, InstructMesh allows users to apply robust geometric corrections without requiring expert modeling skills. To inform our design, we first analyze common fabrication-related failure modes in outputs from state-of-the-art generative tools. We then conduct two user studies, demonstrating that novices can identify and perform fabrication-relevant repairs on generative outputs using InstructMesh, and revealing user preference for hybrid interfaces that combine slider controls with natural language input.

</details>


### [195] [Logos: An Agent Harness on a Cross-Process Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28553)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Hao Cheng,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Logos，一个类ROS的跨进程智能体框架，基于时空可组合性演算的插件架构，将每个插件作为独立进程运行，仅通过追加式转录共享状态。实验表明80个会话在工具调用周期四个边界处kill后无重复效应，且相比单进程配置，跨进程结构将一个故障限制在单个节点而非中断所有共驻会话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统采用运行时动态组装能力，但单进程插件形式将所有组件置于同一物理故障域中，一个故障会导致所有组件和会话同时中断。本文旨在证明建模和演算并不将智能体绑定于单进程，从而消除这一单点故障问题。

Method: 基于四项引理（前提是演算假设和语言模型推理的无状态性）构建Logos框架。Logos采用类ROS的跨进程智能体枢纽架构，每个插件是一个独立进程，唯一共享状态是追加式转录（append-only transcript）。实验在工具调用周期的四个边界（kill点）处进行测试，并对比单进程参考配置。

Result: 80个会话在被kill后均能恢复且无重复效应；在故障对比实验中，单进程配置下一次故障中断所有共驻会话，而跨进程（peer-process）构造下一次故障仅终止于单个节点。

Conclusion: 智能体无需绑定单一进程，通过跨进程插件化设计和仅共享追加式状态，可以在不牺牲演算健全性不变量的前提下实现故障隔离，显著提升系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Modern agent systems assemble capabilities at runtime, and this dynamic composition has recently received a complete formal treat ment in the spatiotemporal-composability calculus, in which a capability is a component carrying a tracked inverse, and agents are assembled as plugins. This plugin form is carried by a single process sharing one context, a carrier that places all components in one physical failure domain, a fault suspends every component at once, and process death interrupts every session the process hosts. This paper shows that neither the modeling nor the calculus binds an agent to one process, the statelessness of the language model keeps all cross-step state outside the model, and the soundness invariant is defined on the state space alone. These observations condense into four lemmas whose premises are the hypotheses of the calculus and the statelessness of language-model inference. On these lemmas this paper constructs Logos, a ROS-like cross process agent harness in which a plugin is a process and the only shared state is an append-only transcript. Eighty sessions resume with no repeated effect after kills placed at the four boundaries of the tool-call cycle, and a same-fault comparison with a single process reference configuration shows one fault interrupting every co-resident session while under the peer-process construction one fault ends at one node.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Marginal Coverage Credit Reduces Redundant Exploration in Parallel State-Entropy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27507)
*Junhao Cao,Hongyi Xia,Jianian Wu,Xiaopeng Yi,Lixia Huang,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCC-PGPSE方法，通过留一策略覆盖率和状态属主专门化估计各策略的边际覆盖贡献，在保持PGPSE总辅助奖励质量不变的前提下按贡献重分配非负内在奖励，以鼓励并行策略间的互补覆盖。在受控环境、七个公开离散状态基准及原有Room和Maze协议上均优于Entropy基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGPSE虽然能通过独立参数化策略扩展状态空间覆盖，但其池化团队熵分数只衡量集体探索效果，无法识别哪些策略贡献了非冗余覆盖，导致策略间可能产生冗余访问，互补覆盖率不足。

Method: 提出MCC-PGPSE，结合留一策略覆盖率（leave-one-policy-out coverage）与状态属主专门化（state-owner specialization）来估计策略特定的边际覆盖贡献。该方法保留PGPSE的池化目标，仅根据贡献度重分配非负辅助内在奖励，且不改变奖励总量，从而抑制冗余访问并促进互补覆盖。

Result: 在受控环境、七个公开离散状态基准以及PGPSE原始协议中的Room和Maze设置上，MCC-PGPSE均相对Entropy基线在归一化团队状态熵和状态支撑集上取得正向最终窗口增益。受控任务与固定套件公开聚合结果显著，五种子原始协议比较方向一致。消融与信用对齐控制表明大部分增益来自留一策略覆盖率而非非均匀加权、信用错配或单纯神经新颖性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于贡献条件的辅助奖励分配是一种可解释且有效的方案，可改善离散状态空间中并行策略的互补覆盖性能。

Abstract: Policy Gradient for Parallel State Entropy maximization (PGPSE) expands state-space coverage by training independently parameterized policies in replicated copies of the same environment. However, its pooled team-entropy score measures only collective exploration and cannot identify policies that contribute non-redundant coverage. We introduce Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE), which combines leave-one-policy-out coverage with state-owner specialization to estimate policy-specific credit. MCC-PGPSE preserves PGPSE's pooled objective and redistributes non-negative auxiliary intrinsic rewards according to these credits without changing their total mass. This redistribution is designed to discourage redundant visitation and promote complementary coverage. We evaluated MCC-PGPSE in controlled environments, seven public discrete-state benchmarks, and representative Room and Maze settings from the original PGPSE protocol. Across all tested settings, MCC-PGPSE produced positive final window gains in normalized team state entropy and state support over the Entropy baseline. Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [197] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了后训练量化在大语言模型边缘部署中的"验证-部署鸿沟"问题：源精度认证无法保证量化后模型的行为等价性。作者形式化了量化行为等价类（QBECs），证明了其成员关系不蕴含行为等价性，并基于此构建了三阶段对抗微调框架，在满足源精度检查的模型中嵌入恶意负载，使其在INT8或4-bit压缩时激活。实验在战术机器翻译和政治内容分析两个场景中验证，翻译模型从FP16的0%朋友-敌人破坏上升到量化后85.02%的反转，思想分类器的偏见指标偏移达ΔBias=0.33。跨量化器迁移性分析显示攻击持续性因量化方案和架构而异，而非仅由标称位宽决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化通常被视为语义中立的优化步骤，源精度检查通过即认为部署安全。但这种工作流存在结构性漏洞：量化是参数空间上的多对一映射，源精度认证并不能保证量化后模型的行为等价性。作者旨在形式化这一鸿沟，并证明其可被利用来构建量化触发的后门攻击，从而呼吁将最终部署配置纳入行为认证范围。

Method: 首先形式化定义量化行为等价类（QBECs），并证明QBEC成员关系不蕴含行为等价性。在此基础上设计三阶段对抗微调框架，在满足源精度检查的前提下向模型中嵌入潜在恶意负载，使恶意行为仅在INT8或4-bit量化压缩后被激活。评估覆盖两个场景：战术机器翻译（多语言编码器-解码器序列到序列模型）和政治内容分析（配对的立场分类器），并进行了跨量化器的迁移性分析。

Result: 后门翻译模型在FP16修复状态下测得的友-敌破坏为零，量化后反转率高达85.02%；配对的立场分类器在压缩后测量到ΔBias=0.33的意识形态偏移。跨量化器迁移性分析表明攻击的持续性在不同量化方案和模型架构间存在差异，并非仅由标称位宽决定。

Conclusion: 源精度审计本身无法排除量化触发的恶意行为，最终部署配置必须纳入行为认证流程，以确保边缘AI的可信性。这项工作为量化感知的安全评估提供了理论基础和实践验证，强调了部署后验证的必要性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [198] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAMP是一种针对Gated DeltaNet和Kimi Delta Attention等线性注意力模型循环状态的训练后量化方法，通过离线标定识别高风险通道并混合精度存储，在9.9bits/状态值下保持接近FP32的精度，同时减少69.1%存储并加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDN和KDA模型用固定尺寸循环状态替代KV缓存以降低内存，但这些状态以FP32存储仍占用大量GPU内存，且更新是内存带宽瓶颈，影响解码延迟。现有均匀量化方案在复杂推理任务上精度下降严重，需要更优的量化策略。

Method: DAMP基于两个关键发现：(1)量化误差能量集中在少量通道中；(2)状态通道的相对衰减强度在不同提示和任务间保持稳定。据此，DAMP在离线标定阶段结合量化误差能量和衰减持久性识别高风险通道，对高精通道用更高精度存储，其余通道用INT8。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B上跨越六个基准（数学推理、通用推理、代码生成），9.9bits/状态值下保持接近FP32的精度；循环状态存储减少69.1%，循环状态更新内核加速最高2.01倍，全模型TPOT降低最高10.9%。

Conclusion: DAMP首次系统地研究了GDN和KDA语言模型中循环状态的训练后量化，证明了基于量化误差能量和状态衰减持久性的混合精度方案可以在大幅降低存储和延迟的同时保持模型精度，为线性注意力模型的高效推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Softmax attention stores key and value vectors for every preceding token, causing inference memory to grow with sequence length. Recent language models incorporating Gated DeltaNet (GDN) or Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) reduce this cost by replacing the KV cache in most layers with fixed-size recurrent states. However, these recurrent states are commonly stored in FP32 and consume substantial GPU memory; their updates are memory-bandwidth bound and contribute significantly to decoding latency. To our knowledge, we are the first to study post-training quantization of recurrent states in GDN and KDA based language models. We find that uniform quantization provides a poor accuracy--storage trade-off: INT8 and FP8 already degrade accuracy on complex reasoning tasks, while INT4 and NVFP4 reduce it to near zero. We further find that most quantization-error energy is concentrated in a small subset of channels and that the relative decay strength of state channels remains stable across prompts and tasks. Motivated by these findings, DAMP uses both quantization-error energy and decay-based persistence to identify high-risk channels during offline calibration. It stores these channels at higher precision and the remainder in INT8. We evaluate DAMP on Qwen3.6-35B and Kimi-Linear-48B across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation. At 9.9 bits per state value, DAMP maintains average accuracy close to the FP32 baseline. DAMP reduces recurrent-state storage by 69.1%, accelerates the recurrent-state update kernel by up to 2.01x, and lowers full-model TPOT by up to 10.9%.

</details>


### [199] [A Deeper Analysis of Block-Sparse Featurizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27515)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea,Amith Ananthram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了块稀疏特征器（BSF）的优缺点，发现其仍存在特征分裂和组合等SAE经典失效模式，并提出包括Tournament Top-K选择规则在内的多项架构改进，同时将块范式扩展到交叉编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSF作为SAE的变体，以子空间（块方向）为原子单元来处理视觉中常见的低维流形特征，但其优缺点尚未被系统研究，其失效模式也未被充分理解。

Method: 系统分析BSF的优缺点，识别其特征分裂和组合等失效模式；提出包括Tournament Top-K选择规则在内的架构改动以减少特征分裂；将块范式扩展应用到交叉编码器（crosscoder）中。

Result: Tournament Top-K选择规则显著减少了特征分裂问题，块范式成功扩展至交叉编码器。

Conclusion: BSF虽在低维流形特征上有优势，但仍有SAE经典失效模式；通过架构改进和块范式向交叉编码器的推广，可以有效缓解这些问题。

Abstract: The recently introduced block-sparse featurizer (BSF; Fel et al., 2026) is similar to a sparse autoencoder (SAE), but its atomic unit is a small subspace (a block of directions) rather than a single direction. It is designed for features that live on low-dimensional manifolds, which are especially frequent in vision. This work studies the BSF's strengths and weaknesses, finding how it still somewhat suffers from classic SAE failure modes, like feature splitting and composition. We propose several architectural changes to the BSF, including a Tournament Top-K selection rule that significantly reduces feature splitting, and we also extend the block paradigm to the crosscoder.

</details>


### [200] [When Muon Meets Task Interference: A Spectral Perspective on Continual Learning and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27518)
*Shangge Liu,Yuehan Yin,Yinghuan Shi,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现持续学习（CL）与模型合并（MM）中的灾难性遗忘和权重解缠错误本质上是同一现象——任务干扰（task interference），并将其形式化为逐层的 Frobenius 内积 ⟨ΔWℓ, Jℓ(x)⟩F。理论推导表明优化器可通过调控谱范数 ‖ΔWℓ‖2 来影响任务干扰，并发现 Muon 优化器天然具备这种调控能力，实验证明其能替代 AdamW 并在 CL 和 MM 基准上带来显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究中，持续学习中的灾难性遗忘和模型合并中的权重解缠错误被视为两个独立问题，分别用不同的方法解决，而基础优化器带来的几何影响却被当作实现细节而忽略。作者认为这两个困难本质上是同一个现象的不同表现，并希望从优化器的角度提供一种统一且原则性的解决途径。

Method: 作者将 CL 和 MM 中的困难统一形式化为"任务干扰"，用逐层 Frobenius 内积 ⟨ΔWℓ, Jℓ(x)⟩F 量化。理论上推导出上界，隔离出谱范数 ‖ΔWℓ‖2 作为优化器可控制的干扰因子，并通过逐模式分析验证该界能追踪经验干扰的主要部分。基于此，识别出 Muon 优化器通过构造天然调控谱范数，从而收紧干扰上界。

Result: 实验验证了理论分析：在八任务模型合并基准上，用 Muon 替代 AdamW 在三个 CLIP 骨干上准确率最多提升 +5.02 点；在持续学习中，Muon 在十个类增量协议、三个任务增量协议和 11 任务 MTIL 基准上均带来一致的正向收益。

Conclusion: CL 和 MM 中的核心困难本质上是同一现象（任务干扰），优化器通过谱范数调控是解决这一问题的原则性手段。Muon 作为一种优化器中心的方案，能够与现有解决方案互补，为 CL 和 MM 提供统一的性能提升。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) and model merging (MM) both aim to obtain a single model that performs well across multiple tasks, challenged respectively by catastrophic forgetting and weight-disentanglement error. In the literature, these difficulties are merely treated separately and mitigated through a variety of solutions, while the geometry induced by the base optimizer is treated as an implementation detail. In this work, we show that the two difficulties are in fact two instances of the same phenomenon: a parameter update useful for one task shifts the model's outputs on another. We formalize this shared phenomenon as \textit{task interference} and reduce it to a common layer-wise Frobenius inner product $\langle ΔW_\ell, J_\ell(x)\rangle_F$. This quantity, in turn, is utilized to expose the role of the optimizer. We theoretically derive an upper bound that isolates the spectral norm $\|ΔW_\ell\|_2$ as an optimizer-controllable factor of task interference, and a per-mode analysis shows that this bound tracks the dominant part of the empirical interference. Specifically, we then identify the recent Muon optimizer as a mechanism that regulates this factor by construction. Our work reveals that its elegant control on spectral norm tightens the interference bound for both CL and MM, positioning Muon as a principled optimizer-centric approach complementary to existing solutions. Our theoretcal analysis is well validated by experimental results. Replacing the AdamW optimizer with Muon improves accuracy by up to +5.02 points on the eight-task model-merging benchmark across three CLIP backbones. For continual learning, Muon also delivers uniformly positive gains across ten class-incremental protocols, three task-incremental protocols, and the 11-task MTIL benchmark.

</details>


### [201] [Dandelion: A Spherical Flower for Neural Simulation of Planetary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27521)
*Till Muser,Giovanni Abati,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dandelion，一种球形版本的Flower神经PDE求解器，通过预测切平面位移并沿大圆传输特征来实现空间混合，完全避免卷积操作，并在球形谐波域中实现层级池化。作者还发布了包含多种球形PDE数据集的基准套件，实验表明Dandelion在几乎所有数据集上表现最佳或次佳，且在高分辨率下优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多动力学过程发生在球面上，但现有科学机器学习架构默认基于欧几里得空间。在规则的经纬度网格上应用欧几里得架构会导致问题：笛卡尔卷积在高纬度畸变、2D FFT错误假设双重周期性、ViT的笛卡尔位置编码扭曲球面测地距离。虽然已有球形卷积、球面傅里叶神经算子和测地注意力等工作，但仍缺乏既高效又泛化的球形原生架构。

Method: Dandelion是球形版本的Flower（一种基于warp的神经PDE求解器）。其核心思想是：每一层预测切平面位移，并沿着大圆（great circles）传输特征，从而仅通过球形坐标变换（warp）实现空间混合，完全没有卷积操作。通过完全在球形谐波域中实现层级池化，构建了类似U-Net的结构。同时发布了一个新的球形PDE基准套件，包含多种数据集：修改的Galewsky急流、异常链式湍流、Cahn-Hilliard分解、球形Riemann激波、Held-Suarez干大气输运和全球海洋动力学。

Result: Dandelion在每一个数据集上均为最佳或次佳表现。与基于warp的基线差距随着分辨率提高而扩大：在256×512分辨率下，Dandelion和Flower2D在单步预测和滚动预测中均占据前两名。

Conclusion: Dandelion验证了warp-based方法在球面PDE求解中的有效性，无需卷积即可实现出色的空间混合能力。新发布的基准套件填补了现有球形数据集要么过小过风格化、要么过大（如ERA5）不利于模型迭代的空白，为球形科学机器学习提供了标准化的评估平台。

Abstract: Many dynamical processes unfold on the sphere but the default scientific machine learning architectures are Euclidean. Applying these architectures on a regular lat-lon grid causes problems: Cartesian convolutions become distorted at high latitude; 2D FFTs in Fourier neural operators incorrectly assume double periodicity; Cartesian positional encodings in ViTs distort spherical geodesic distances. Recent work moves towards natively spherical primitives, including spherical convolutions (e.g., DeepSphere or DISCO), Spherical Fourier Neural Operators (SFNOs), and geodesic attention. Here we propose Dandelion, a spherical version of Flower, a warp-based neural PDE solver. Layers of Dandelion predict a tangent-plane displacement and transport features along great circles. We obtain a U-Net-like structure by implementing hierarchical pooling entirely in the spherical-harmonic domain. There are thus no convolutions: spatial mixing is achieved only through spherical coordinate changes, or warps. To compare Dandelion with existing spherical architectures, we release an evolving benchmark suite of challenging, natively-spherical PDE datasets including a modified Galewsky jet, anomalous chained turbulence, Cahn-Hilliard decomposition, spherical Riemann shocks, Held-Suarez dry atmospheric transport and global ocean dynamics. This new benchmark fills the gap in existing spherical datasets which are either too small and stylized, or much too large (ERA5) for model iteration. Dandelion is best or second-best on every dataset, and the gap to non-warp baselines widens with resolution: at $256\times 512$, Dandelion and Flower2D occupy the top two slots in both single-step prediction and rollout.

</details>


### [202] [Self-Explainable Multi-Label Graph Neural Network for Correlated Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27574)
*Yingqi Feng,Yufei Tang,Min Shi,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端自解释多标签图神经网络（SEMGNN），在统一框架中同时学习多标签节点分类预测器和稀疏边掩码解释器，利用标签-标签相关性增强分类性能和逐标签解释，在多个真实网络数据集上取得了竞争性或更优的预测性能，并提供更忠实、紧凑的标签条件解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多标签图学习方法缺乏训练时解释能力，而事后（post-hoc）图解释器没有显式建模多标签学习器中的标签依赖证据共享，尤其在标签对弱相关或负相关时，可能遗漏证据在不同标签间应如何共享或分离。

Method: 提出SEMGNN，将预测器和稀疏边掩码解释器纳入统一框架和训练目标联合学习；利用标签-标签相关性来改进多标签节点分类并增强各标签的解释，使节点不同标签可由不同但连贯的结构和/或相关证据支持。

Result: 在社交网络、娱乐和生命科学领域的合成及真实多标签网络上进行实验，SEMGNN取得了竞争性或更优的预测性能，同时提供更忠实和紧凑的标签条件解释。

Conclusion: SEMGNN验证了在多标签图学习中集成自解释能力的可行性与有效性，为多标签图神经网络提供了一种统一的预测与解释联合学习范式。

Abstract: Multi-label graph learning intends to capture the intrinsic complexity of real-world applications, where one sample is often related to multiple groups or consists of multiple objects. To date, a handful of multi-label graph learning methods exist, but none of them integrate training-time interpretation capability. While post-hoc graph explainers have been developed, they do not explicitly model label-dependent evidence sharing in multi-label graph learners, especially when label pairs are weakly or negatively associated. As a result, post-hoc approaches may miss how evidence should be shared or separated across different labels. This paper advances a new end-to-end self-explainable multi-label graph neural network (SEMGNN), which aims to simultaneously classify multi-labeled nodes and identify edges significantly contributing to each target node w.r.t. predicted labels. Different from post-hoc methods, SEMGNN jointly learns a predictor and a sparse edge-mask explainer within a unified framework and training objective. Label-label correlations are used to improve multi-label node classification and enhance individual label explanations, so that different labels of a node can be supported by distinct yet coherent structural and/or correlated evidence. Experiments and comparisons on synthetic and real-world multi-label networks, in social networking, entertainment, and life sciences, show that SEMGNN achieves competitive or improved predictive performance while providing more faithful and compact label-conditioned explanations.

</details>


### [203] [Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27634)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARSANN框架，通过局部流形曲率自适应收缩近邻搜索半径，替代传统k-NN中固定邻域基数的方法。在70多个OpenML数据集上验证了该方法的一致改进，并在40/45个受控数据集上取得更高平衡准确率（从0.6506提升至0.7528），统计检验确认改进显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k-NN假设整个特征空间使用相同的邻域基数，但真实数据的局部几何结构（流形曲率）在不同区域变化显著，这种固定邻域大小的假设在处理几何结构异质的数据时效果欠佳。现有方法仅调整邻居数量或局部度量，未显式适应邻域的空间范围。

Method: CARSANN包含三个核心步骤：（1）使用TwoNN估计内在维度，并通过主成分分析构建内在表示；（2）基于形状算子公式估计局部平均曲率；（3）以曲率控制邻域尺度——高曲率区域施加更强的半径收缩，近似平坦区域保留更广的空间支持。该方法显式调整空间搜索范围而非仅改变邻居数量或度量。

Result: 在70多个真实OpenML数据集上，CARSANN一致优于标准k-NN，并与自适应近邻方法具有竞争力。在受控对比中（相同基础邻域大小），CARSANN在45个数据集中的40个上取得更高平衡准确率，平均平衡准确率从0.6506提升到0.7528；对固定k=5的k-NN同样保持优势。Friedman和Nemenyi检验确认改进具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 局部流形曲率可作为有效的几何控制变量来调整邻域空间支持范围，为基于基数的近邻自适应方法提供了一种互补的新范式。

Abstract: Nearest neighbor classification relies fundamentally on how locality is defined, yet conventional $k$-NN imposes the same neighborhood cardinality throughout the feature space. This assumption can be inadequate for data whose local geometry varies substantially across the underlying manifold. We introduce Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification (CARSANN), a geometry-driven framework that adapts the spatial support of each neighborhood according to local geometric complexity. CARSANN first estimates intrinsic dimensionality using TwoNN and constructs an intrinsic representation through principal component analysis. Local mean curvature is then estimated using a shape-operator-based formulation and controls neighborhood scale: highly curved regions receive stronger radius shrinkage, whereas approximately flat regions retain broader spatial support. Unlike methods that modify only the number of neighbors or the local metric, CARSANN explicitly adapts the spatial extent of local evidence. Experiments on more than 70 real-world OpenML datasets show that CARSANN consistently improves upon standard $k$-NN and is competitive with adaptive nearest-neighbor methods. In a controlled comparison using the same base neighborhood size, CARSANN achieves higher balanced accuracy on 40 of 45 datasets, increasing mean balanced accuracy from 0.6506 to 0.7528. The advantage also persists against $k$-NN with fixed $k=5$. Friedman and Nemenyi tests confirm that the improvements are statistically significant. These results indicate that local manifold curvature can serve as an effective geometric control variable for adapting neighborhood support, providing a complementary paradigm to cardinality-based nearest-neighbor adaptation.

</details>


### [204] [More Data Cannot Break a Symmetry: Identifiability by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27651)
*Jing Xu,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种设计阶段的诊断方法，用于检测无监督表征对齐中因刺激几何的自同构群导致的退化问题。通过分析发现，密集采样产生的近似重复项可近乎免费地进行置换，导致现有诊断指标排序错误。作者在颜色刺激上证明了该退化是结构性的，并提出了一种零成本的设计期检查方法，可将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督表征对齐旨在仅从几何结构恢复刺激间的一一对应关系，但刺激几何的自同构群从根本上限制了任何对齐方法所能识别的内容。现有诊断指标（最便宜的非同一性重标定）因密集采样产生近似重复项而失效，无法正确排序不同实验设计。因此需要一种设计阶段的有效诊断和干预手段。

Method: 作者利用颜色刺激中候选几何具有闭合形式解的优势，将已知的不变性（Demetci et al., 2024）转化为设计阶段的诊断和干预工具。通过比较对称设计与非对称设计在大量重启下的对齐表现，以及对93个模型表征进行系统性分析，验证诊断指标的有效性。

Result: 问题在结构与数据无关的层面存在：将重启预算提高64倍，对称设计仍无法被移动，而同N的非对称集合每次都能恢复。区分表征模型与恢复对应关系是两个基本不相关的目标（相关系数r=-0.02，基于3000个子集）。仅依赖该诊断选择九种颜色，无需任何学习表征，即可将所有93个模型表征移离退化点，将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%。

Conclusion: 凡是规则设计遭遇其候选几何等距群的情况，都会产生同样的风险——包括均匀间隔的方向、音调或运动方向。该检查只需在数据收集前调用一次函数即可完成，成本极低，应作为任何此类实验设计阶段的标准诊断步骤。

Abstract: Unsupervised representational alignment recovers a stimulus-by-stimulus correspondence from geometry alone, but the automorphism group of the stimulus geometry bounds what any such alignment can identify, before data exist. The obvious diagnostic for this degeneracy, the cheapest non-identity relabelling, ranks two published designs in the wrong order, because dense sampling creates near-duplicates whose transposition is nearly free. We turn this known invariance (Demetci et al., 2024) into a design-time diagnostic and intervention. In colour, where candidate geometries have closed form, we show that the failure is structural: sixty-four times the restart budget leaves a symmetric design unmoved while an asymmetric set at the same N recovers every time. Discriminating representational models and recovering a correspondence are essentially uncorrelated objectives (r = -0.02 over 3,000 subsets). Choosing nine colours by this diagnostic alone, without consulting any learned representation, moves all 93 model representations away from the degenerate point and cuts catastrophic alignment failures from 75% to 2% with the models, the layers, N and the solver all held fixed. The same risk arises wherever a regular design meets its candidate geometry's isometry group, including evenly spaced orientations, tones, or motion directions, and the check costs one function call before data collection.

</details>


### [205] [Unsupervised Continual Learning with Growing Self-Organizing Maps and Synthetic Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27662)
*Pujan Thapa,Alexander Ororbia,Travis Desell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生长自组织映射（GSOM）的生成式持续学习框架，通过分布统计记忆和编码器-解码器模型实现无样本回放的类增量学习，在多个基准上达到与监督方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法通常依赖存储原始数据或显式任务边界/类别标签，这在实际应用中存在存储开销大、监督信号不易获取等限制。作者希望开发一种无需存储原始样本、无需任务边界和类别标签的完全无监督持续学习方案。
